{"forms":[{"form":"harmonic means","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The harmonic mean of two numbers a and c is the number b such that #123;a-b#92;overa#125;#61;#123;b-c#92;overc#125;. Combining the two ratios: #123;b-c#92;overc#125;#61;#123;(a-b)#43;(b-c)#92;overa#43;c#125;#61;#123;a-c#92;overa#43;c#125;. Rotating the proportion 90° clockwise: #123;c#92;overa#43;c#125;#61;#123;b-c#92;overa-c#125;; transposing twice to isolate the b: b#61;c#43;c#92;left(#123;a-c#92;overa#43;c#125;#92;right). Likewise b#61;a-a#92;left(#123;a-c#92;overa#43;c#125;#92;right); these are both equivalent to the more typical, symmetrical equations: b#61;#123;2ac#92;overa#43;c#125;#61;#123;2#92;over#123;1#92;overa#125;#43;#123;1#92;overc","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A type of measure of central tendency calculated as the reciprocal of the mean of the reciprocals, i.e., H=n/1/x_1+1/x_2+⋯+1/x_n."],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[],"upstream_line":509887,"upstream_offset":2361862003,"upstream_record_sha256":"3ee1d2f7a1b3bfc975481f6ff7def270cff658d19205fe4814f1074f985fa3e6","word":"harmonic mean"}
